Load before or during design work, especially when a design choice is non-obvious. ([AlexanderVegazo26/agents](https://github.com/AlexanderVegazo26/agents/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ux-research) · 7 copies)
- **ux-research**: User research — interviews, personas, journey maps, usability testing, synthesis ([karvifi/OS](https://github.com/karvifi/OS/tree/HEAD/skills/ux-research) · 2 copies)
- **ux-research**: Génère des guides d'entretien utilisateur et des questionnaires UX complets en français, adaptés à un contexte entreprise (clients et conseillers internes). À utiliser dès qu'un utilisateur mentionne : entretien utilisateur, guide d'entretien, questionnaire UX, sondage utilisateur, research plan, protocole de recherche, capa à valider, user story à tester, feedback utilisateur, test de concept, ou tout travail de cadrage UX en amont d'un projet produit. Déclencher ce skill même si la demande est formulée de façon informelle (ex: "j'ai une capa à investiguer", "je dois interviewer des clients… ([leandrodslv/claude-skills-library](https://github.com/leandrodslv/claude-skills-library/tree/HEAD/importes/design/ux-research/.claude/skills/ux-research))
- **ux-research**: When to activate: user research, UX research, user interviews, usability testing, surveys, diary studies, research plan, synthesis, insight ([Mattakushi432/Claude-Code-Skills-Custom-DevTools-Pack](https://github.com/Mattakushi432/Claude-Code-Skills-Custom-DevTools-Pack/tree/HEAD/plugins/devtools-pack/skills/ux-research))
- **ux-research**: Plan and run usability studies and behavioural analysis, then turn findings into research-backed personas. Use when running usability tests, journey mapping, or observing user behavior. ([poorvith-mp/skills-design](https://github.com/poorvith-mp/skills-design/tree/HEAD/skills/ux-research))
- **ux-research**: Conduct user research — interviews, surveys, usability testing, personas, journey mapping — to ground design decisions in real user behavior. ([quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk](https://github.com/quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ux/ux-research))
- **ux-research**: Conduct user research, analyze behavior patterns, create personas, map user journeys, and synthesize insights into actionable recommendations. Use when understanding users, validating designs, or making data-driven UX decisions. ([roborracle/agent-workflow-kit](https://github.com/roborracle/agent-workflow-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/ux-research))
- **ux-research**: Online research on a UX/UI problem (a screen, flow, component, or interaction) — fans out parallel subagents to study established patterns, real-world examples, antipatterns, accessibility, and mobile/responsive considerations, then presents 3–6 distinct UI variations as live rendered HTML/CSS mockups inside a single standalone dark-themed HTML report. Each variation includes a rationale, when-to-use guidance, and cited real-world inspirations. Writes the report to `/tmp/claude-research/` and opens it in the default browser. Use when the user invokes `/ux-research` or `/ui-research`, or says… ([spacehub-mn/spacehub-skills](https://github.com/spacehub-mn/spacehub-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/research-toolkit/skills/ux-research))
- **ux-research-and-usability-testing**: Use when planning moderated usability sessions, field research with users and community stakeholders, participant tasks, observation evidence, interviews, surveys, card/tree tests, diary studies, or findings synthesis. Do not use for expert-only heuristic inspection or unsupported personas; route those to heuristic evaluation or journey mapping. ([peterbamuhigire/chwezi-design-engine](https://github.com/peterbamuhigire/chwezi-design-engine/tree/HEAD/skills/05-ux-process-research-and-psychology/ux-research-and-usability-testing) · ★5)
- **ux-research-deck**: Build a UX research deck outline — 6-slide outcome deck (default) or 12–18 slide full deck — and render a real .pptx on a template the user provides (BK Charterline ships no theme; ask for the .pptx/.potx first). Invoke after synthesis when the user wants slides. ([BojanKocijan/bk-charterline](https://github.com/BojanKocijan/bk-charterline/tree/HEAD/skills/ux-research-deck) · ★4)
- **ux-research-design-toolkit**: 当需要把用户研究数据转化为可落地的设计决策时使用；做用户画像生成、旅程地图绘制、可用性测试规划与研究综合（产出画像/旅程/测试方案/洞察）；不适用于视觉 UI 组件库设计、纯前端实现或无任何用户数据的拍脑袋假设。触发词：用户画像、旅程地图、可用性测试、研究综合、痛点分析。 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/06-creative/ux-research-design-toolkit) · ★3)
- **ux-research-director**: Reviews Director-level UX research practice for cross-product research operations, method-to-decision fit, and insight advocacy. Activates when the user is deciding which research method fits a decision, building or scaling a research practice from scratch, designing ReOps systems (repository, governance, democratization), diagnosing why research findings are not shipping decisions, or evaluating senior UXR hiring or leveling. Defaults to skepticism of surveys, focus groups, and "validate-the-design" framings (Erika Hall lineage). Does not handle single-study execution craft (defer to IC rese… ([PlevanTem/luban-skill](https://github.com/PlevanTem/luban-skill/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ux-research-director))
- **ux-research-discovery-testing**: Use when planning UX research, discovery interviews, usability tests, synthesis, or evidence-backed product recommendations. ([hueyexe/frontend-agent-skills](https://github.com/hueyexe/frontend-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/ux-research-discovery-testing) · ★13)
- **ux-research-guide**: Guide de recherche UX : interviews, tests utilisateurs, personas et analyse. Se déclenche avec "UX research", "recherche utilisateur", "interview utilisateur", "test utilisateur", "persona", "user testing", "usability", "parcours utilisateur". Also triggers on "user interviews", "usability testing". ([khalilbenaz/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/khalilbenaz/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/dev-skills/ux-research-guide) · ★22)
- **ux-research-guide**: UX research methodology — transcript ingestion, PII redaction, per-call summaries, thematic clustering, RICE + MoSCoW, JTBD, 6-slide outcome deck / 12–18 slide full deck. Invoke on "research mode", "analyze transcripts", or any qualitative research request. To plan a study (an interview guide, a usability test script, a survey), use design:user-research when installed; without it, plan the study here and say the plugin would help. This skill leads once there are transcripts. ([BojanKocijan/bk-charterline](https://github.com/BojanKocijan/bk-charterline/tree/HEAD/skills/ux-research-guide) · ★4)
